# G313 audit report — trace-free Ricci solution space and bootstrap boundary

Date: 2026-09-01
Status: `INTERNAL_VERIFIED_LEAD__EXTERNAL_ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW_PENDING`

## Landing

```text
ACTIVE_EQUATION_DEFINES_MULTIBRANCH_EINSTEIN_ARENA
__GLOBAL_ADMISSIBILITY_REMAINS_OPEN
```

The adopted G312 premises plus Universal Reciprocity activate a genuine local vacuum spacetime law
inside their bounded arena. Bianchi completes it to `Ric_ab=Lambda g_ab` with one constant `Lambda`
per connected regular region.

That is not a unique spacetime. The equation leaves the Weyl/tidal channel, initial data, topology,
global completion, and scalar magnitude open. Exact witnesses include:

- the positive round cosh history;
- a smooth compact positive `dS2 x S2` product with nonzero Weyl curvature;
- non-round Berger-`S3` Cauchy data satisfying the same fixed-positive-`Lambda` constraints;
- Ricci-flat plane waves with nonzero tidal curvature;
- positive, zero, and negative scalar sectors;
- a constant-homothety family that preserves the equation while rescaling `Lambda`.

Therefore the round cosh solution is a distinguished controlled branch, not the complete active
solution space. No metric, reciprocal kernel, angular cancellation, or observational interface was
changed.

## Remaining bridge

A bootstrap compatible with Local Metric Sufficiency must be a diffeomorphism-invariant predicate
on complete histories. It may select which metric exists globally; it may not add a separate hidden
remote-history response after the admitted local metric jet is fixed. No currently owned UDT
premise supplies a nonidentity predicate of that type.

## Evidence

- outcome-blind preregistration pushed at `e0b6e478`;
- 72 exact standard-library production assertions;
- 58 implementation-distinct standard-library assertions;
- direct Koszul reconstruction of non-round Berger data;
- independent closed Berger formulas and tensor homothety check;
- exact Weyl-invariant separation of round and product Einstein histories;
- eight of eight semantic hostile mutations caught;
- no observation, fit, source, action, matter, mass, scale, physical `X_max`, or protected input.

## Scope boundary

This is a structural census, not an enumeration of all Einstein manifolds. Global stability,
singular/characteristic boundaries, matter/source extensions, topology change, physical population,
and observational calibration remain open. Standard local Cauchy development is a conditional
mathematical method already scoped by G303, not a newly imported UDT field equation.

# G313 exact derivation — the active equation is a multi-branch arena

Date: 2026-09-01
Scope: regular local smooth metric-only scale-free vacuum, with explicitly stated global witnesses

## 1. The adopted bounded equation

Write

\[
S_{ab}=R_{ab}-\frac14R g_{ab}.
\]

The owner-adopted provisional premises activate `S_ab=0` only inside G312's registered bounded
arena. Contracted Bianchi gives

\[
\nabla^aS_{ab}
=\left(\frac12-\frac14\right)\nabla_bR
=\frac14\nabla_bR=0.
\]

Therefore, on each connected regular solution region,

\[
R=4\Lambda,\qquad d\Lambda=0,\qquad R_{ab}=\Lambda g_{ab}.
\]

This fixes a local vacuum response law. It does not fix the value of `Lambda`, Cauchy data, global
topology, or which solution is physically populated.

## 2. Constant curvature is a proper subset

For a four-dimensional Einstein metric,

\[
R_{abcd}=C_{abcd}
+\frac{\Lambda}{3}(g_{ac}g_{bd}-g_{ad}g_{bc}).
\]

The equation fixes the Ricci channel but leaves the Weyl tensor `C_abcd` free subject to its own
geometric identities and evolution. Only the subfamily `C_abcd=0` has constant sectional curvature.
Thus trace-free Ricci does not imply that every solution is round de Sitter, flat Minkowski, or
anti-de Sitter.

For the constant-curvature subfamily,

\[
R_{abcd}R^{abcd}=\frac83\Lambda^2,
\qquad C_{abcd}C^{abcd}=0.
\]

The positive round G309 solution

\[
a(T)=X\cosh\!\left(\frac{T-T_0}{X}\right),
\qquad \Lambda=\frac3{X^2},
\]

is one exact member of this conformally flat subfamily.

## 3. Exact positive Weyl-active compact witness

For `Lambda>0`, consider the product metric

\[
ds^2=\Lambda^{-1}
\left[-d\tau^2+\cosh^2\tau\,d\chi^2+d\Omega_2^2\right],
\qquad \chi\sim\chi+2\pi.
\]

Each two-dimensional factor has `Ric=Lambda g`, so the product has

\[
R_{ab}=\Lambda g_{ab},\qquad R=4\Lambda.
\]

Its curvature invariants are

\[
R_{abcd}R^{abcd}=8\Lambda^2,
\qquad
C_{abcd}C^{abcd}=\frac{16}{3}\Lambda^2\ne0.
\]

It is smooth, time-live, and has compact `S1 x S2` Cauchy slices. It satisfies the same active local
equation as the round positive history but is neither round nor constant-curvature. This exact
witness alone rejects the preregistered unique-round-history landing. It does not claim that this
history is physically populated.

## 4. Same-topology non-round Cauchy data

The Gauss--Codazzi constraints for `Ric=Lambda g` are

\[
{}^{(3)}R+K^2-K_{ij}K^{ij}=2\Lambda,
\qquad
D^j(K_{ij}-\gamma_{ij}K)=0.
\]

For pure-trace homogeneous data `K_ij=h gamma_ij`, these reduce to

\[
{}^{(3)}R+6h^2=2\Lambda,
\qquad M_i=0.
\]

Fix `Lambda=3`. The round unit `S3` bounce has `R3=6` and `h=0`. Now take the Berger `S3` metric
whose left-invariant orthonormal lengths are `(1,1,3/2)`. A direct Koszul calculation gives

\[
\operatorname{Ric}^{(3)}
=\operatorname{diag}\!\left(-\frac12,-\frac12,\frac92\right),
\qquad {}^{(3)}R=\frac72.
\]

It is non-round. Choosing

\[
h^2=\frac5{12}
\]

gives `R3+6h^2=6=2Lambda`; homogeneity makes the momentum constraint vanish. G303's explicitly
conditional standard local Cauchy theorem then supplies a local development. Hence even fixed
positive `Lambda` and `S3` topology do not force the round cosh data.

At the time-symmetric slice of the product witness, `R3=6` and `K_ij=0` at the same `Lambda=3`, but
the topology is `S1 x S2`. The equation therefore leaves both shape data and topology open.

## 5. Zero and negative branches

The scalar sign is not selected by the equation. For `Lambda=0`, the exact plane-wave family

\[
ds^2=-2\,du\,dv+dx^2+dy^2+A(x^2-y^2)du^2
\]

has

\[
R_{uu}=-\frac12(H_{,xx}+H_{,yy})=0
\]

while retaining nonzero tidal/Weyl curvature for `A!=0`. Negative Einstein and Weyl-active product
sectors are likewise algebraically admitted. Their global boundary and causality completions must
be classified separately; G313 does not promote one.

## 6. The equation cannot set its own absolute scale

Under a constant homothety

\[
\widetilde g_{ab}=s^2g_{ab},
\]

the Levi--Civita connection and lower-index Ricci tensor are unchanged, while

\[
\widetilde R=s^{-2}R,
\qquad
\widetilde R_{ab}-\frac14\widetilde R\widetilde g_{ab}
=R_{ab}-\frac14Rg_{ab}.
\]

Thus `Lambda` transforms to `s^-2 Lambda`. The equation selects neither its magnitude nor an
absolute length. A curvature radius is a parameter of a supplied solution, not automatically
physical `X_max`.

## 7. Exact type of the remaining bootstrap bridge

Let `Sol` be the class of complete histories admitted by the local equation and the declared
regularity/causal scope. A compatible global bootstrap has the type

\[
\mathcal A:\operatorname{Sol}/\operatorname{Diff}\longrightarrow\{0,1\}.
\]

It is a diffeomorphism-invariant acceptance predicate on entire histories. It may test topology,
global completion, boundary data, relation-network consistency, or another whole-history property.
This does not violate Local Metric Sufficiency: after `A` accepts a metric, the local vacuum
response is still determined by that metric's finite jet. The forbidden alternative would be two
accepted histories with identical admitted local jets but different local responses solely because
of an extra hidden remote-history label.

No existing UDT premise supplies a nonidentity instance of `A`. W5/W6 and the relation network type
and evaluate histories; G305--G308 characterize the round positive Hopf branch but do not declare
that branch physically populated or reject the other Einstein branches.

## 8. Scientific landing

```text
ACTIVE_EQUATION_DEFINES_MULTIBRANCH_EINSTEIN_ARENA
__GLOBAL_ADMISSIBILITY_REMAINS_OPEN
```

The earlier “who writes phi?” problem has split cleanly:

1. the adopted bounded local equation now propagates complete metric data;
2. initial/boundary data and a still-open global admissibility rule choose a particular history;
3. observation may later calibrate or discriminate surviving constants, but is not used here.

`phi` is a presentation potential extracted from a supplied metric or completed pair. It is not one
universal radial function common to all solutions of the covariant equation.

# G313 preregistration — trace-free Ricci solution space and bootstrap boundary

Date: 2026-09-01
Question type: `METRIC_LED__OBSERVING_NOT_TARGETING`
Status: `PREREGISTERED_BEFORE_OUTCOMES`

## Whole question

After Charles's provisional adoption of Universal Reciprocity and both G312 bridge premises, the
registered regular local smooth metric-only scale-free vacuum arena obeys

```text
Ric_ab-(R/4)g_ab=0.
```

What solution-space structure does this equation actually own, and do the existing UDT relational,
causal, projective, completion, and Hopf results select the positive round cosh history or merely
evaluate one member of a larger arena? What exact mathematical type must a lawful global bootstrap
or admissibility condition have if local vacuum response remains metric-sufficient?

This is not a search for a preferred cosmology. It is a census of what the adopted bounded equation
admits and what the existing premises still fail to select.

## Premise ledger

### `pinned-by-THEORY`

- completed Lorentzian metric as the physical local geometry;
- owner-adopted provisional Universal Reciprocity / DDR;
- owner-adopted provisional full quiet-GR principal-response overlap;
- owner-adopted provisional Local Metric Sufficiency;
- the G312 bounded regular local smooth metric-only scale-free vacuum arena;
- standard tensor identities and product/pullback geometry as mathematical method;
- G303's conditional local Cauchy formulation for each fixed connected scalar sector.

### `free-and-explored`

- scalar-curvature sign and magnitude;
- conformally flat versus Weyl-active geometry;
- static, homogeneous time-live, and exact product/plane-wave witness sectors;
- compact versus noncompact spatial topology;
- initial data, global completion, and constant homothety;
- whether a global bootstrap/admissibility predicate accepts or rejects a complete history.

### `pinned-by-HABIT`

- none.

### Explicitly omitted

- matter, sources, actions, mass laws, stability claims, observations, fits, optical transfer,
  calibrated scales, physical `X_max`, and protected local work;
- singular or distributional response laws;
- a claim to enumerate every Einstein manifold up to isometry;
- a claim that mathematical existence means physical population.

## Preregistered class map

The derivation must test all of the following structural classes rather than only the known round
positive branch:

1. connected-scalar reduction by contracted Bianchi;
2. positive, zero, and negative scalar sectors;
3. conformally flat (`Weyl=0`) constant-curvature sector;
4. Weyl-active (`Weyl!=0`) Einstein sector;
5. constant-homothety/absolute-scale freedom;
6. local Cauchy-data freedom and its constraint equations;
7. compact round `S3` branch already controlled by G304--G309;
8. at least one exact non-round or non-constant-curvature witness admitted by the same equation;
9. global admissibility as a diffeomorphism-invariant predicate on complete histories, separated
   from the local response equation.

## Candidate landings

Exactly one primary landing will be selected without changing the tests after outcomes:

1. `ACTIVE_EQUATION_AND_EXISTING_UDT_SELECT_UNIQUE_ROUND_HISTORY`;
2. `ACTIVE_EQUATION_DEFINES_MULTIBRANCH_EINSTEIN_ARENA__GLOBAL_ADMISSIBILITY_REMAINS_OPEN`;
3. `ACTIVE_EQUATION_HAS_NO_REGULAR_NONTRIVIAL_HISTORY_IN_REGISTERED_SCOPE`;
4. `CERTIFICATION_FAILURE__NO_SCIENTIFIC_LANDING`.

## Required derivations and witnesses

- derive `dR=0` and `Ric=Lambda g` from the active equation without importing a field equation;
- decompose four-dimensional curvature into Weyl plus the constant-curvature channel;
- verify the round positive cosh member as one conformally flat solution, not assume uniqueness;
- independently verify an exact Weyl-active Einstein witness;
- derive the constant-homothety action and determine whether it fixes the scalar magnitude;
- state the Gauss--Codazzi constraint surface for supplied Cauchy data and distinguish a law from a
  particular solution;
- formalize a compatible global admissibility predicate that selects complete metrics without
  adding a hidden remote-history response after the local metric jet is fixed;
- test whether any existing UDT premise supplies a nonidentity instance of that predicate.

## Falsification contract

The round-history uniqueness landing is rejected if one regular exact metric outside the round
constant-curvature family satisfies the active equation and all premises actually applied to that
witness. The multi-branch landing is rejected if the active equation plus already-owned UDT gates
forces `Weyl=0`, fixes the scalar magnitude, or uniquely fixes the Cauchy data. A bootstrap claim is
rejected unless a currently owned nonidentity predicate distinguishes two otherwise admitted
complete histories without changing their local response law.

Hostile checks must catch at least:

- replacing `1/4` by a wrong trace coefficient;
- asserting Bianchi fixes the numerical scalar rather than only its connected constancy;
- asserting every Einstein metric is constant curvature;
- erasing the Weyl-active witness;
- identifying a constant-curvature radius with physical `X_max`;
- calling the round cosh branch the unique active universe;
- treating Local Metric Sufficiency as a ban on global bootstrap;
- treating an admissibility predicate as an extra local memory response.

## Ten-criterion coverage

1. **Fields:** complete metric only in the registered vacuum arena; no matter.
2. **Action terms:** none selected; the equation is postulate-owned in bounded scope.
3. **Full equations:** the full covariant trace-free Ricci equation plus Bianchi completion.
4. **Domain/coordinates:** coordinate-free local class, with explicitly bounded exact witnesses.
5. **Boundary/regularity:** regular local and stated global witnesses; arbitrary boundaries open.
6. **Topology:** round `S3`, a distinct compact product topology, and noncompact witnesses sampled;
   no topology selected.
7. **Dynamics:** full time-live Cauchy character retained; no static-only verdict.
8. **Branches:** scalar sign and Weyl branches explicitly separated.
9. **Stability:** omitted and open.
10. **Regime:** regular local smooth metric-only scale-free vacuum; sources, singularities, and
    matter are outside scope.

This package is one structural tile, not a complete census of all global Einstein manifolds.

## Certification and maximum conclusion

Production and independent implementations must share no load-bearing formula code. Exact or
rational identities are preferred. All registered hostile semantic mutations must fail. The full
premise verifier and repository suite must pass before banking, followed by a fresh read-only
adversarial review.

Maximum conclusion: a bounded structural map of the active vacuum solution space and the exact
remaining global selection type. No law, history, scale, observation, mass, source, action,
physical `X_max`, or canon entry may be selected by this package.

# G312 exact derivation — quiet-GR response-constitution discriminator

Date: 2026-09-01
Status: `EXTERNALLY_ACCEPTED_WITH_TWO_PREMISE_BOUNDARY`

## 1. Landing

```text
TWO_OR_MORE_INDEPENDENT_NEW_PREMISES_ARE_REQUIRED
```

This is not a return to an unrestricted formula search. Existing UDT structure leaves exactly two
independent architecture choices load-bearing in this bounded lane:

1. `FULL_QUIET_GR_PRINCIPAL_RESPONSE_OVERLAP`;
2. `LOCAL_FINITE_JET_RESPONSE_CONSTITUTION`.

If both are provisionally supplied, the active no-new-operator-scale, quiet-regular, metric-only
gates close the response to G301, and G311 then gives trace-free Ricci. Neither clause is currently
derived or owner-adopted in that strengthened form.

## 2. What G311 already fixes

Inside G301, a natural response has

\[
E_{ab}=aR_{ab}+bR g_{ab}.
\]

Its trace-free part is exactly

\[
\operatorname{TF}_g(E)_{ab}
=a\left(R_{ab}-\frac14R g_{ab}\right)=aS_{ab}.
\]

For the nondegenerate quiet-GR branch, \(a\ne0\). Universal Reciprocity therefore yields

\[
S_{ab}=0.
\]

The coefficient \(b\) is irrelevant to this response-shape equation. The unresolved issue is not
which coefficient in G301 to fit; it is whether the physical response belongs to G301 at all.

The production census verifies this identity on 128 exact rational symmetric tensors and arbitrary
rational \((a,b)\), totaling 2,048 component identities. The independent flattened-tensor census
checks 1,920 more identities with a different representation.

## 3. Why the G301 ray theorem is exact

Let \(K\) be algebraic curvature and \(F(K)\) the response. If

\[
F(tK)=tF(K),\qquad t>0,
\]

and \(F\) is differentiable at the quiet origin with \(F(0)=0\), then

\[
DF_0(K)=\lim_{t\to0^+}\frac{F(tK)-F(0)}t=F(K).
\]

Thus \(F=DF_0\) and is exactly linear in curvature. G301's externally certified Lorentz-natural
basis census then leaves only \(R_{ab}\) and \(Rg_{ab}\).

The quiet differentiability assumption matters. A nonlinear degree-one direction map such as

\[
H(x,y)=\frac{(x^3,y^3)}{x^2+y^2}
\]

is scale-free away from zero, but its directional derivatives are not additive and it is undefined
at the quiet origin.

## 4. Solution overlap is weaker than GR response overlap

Consider the regular local curvature-quadratic response

\[
Q_{ab}=\operatorname{TF}_g\!\left(R_{ac}R_b{}^c\right).
\]

Every Ricci-flat GR metric satisfies \(Q_{ab}=0\). Hence it passes **solution overlap**. But

\[
Q[tR]=t^2Q[R],
\]

so its first variation at flat curvature vanishes. It has no GR quiet principal operator. Exact
rational production and independent witnesses verify both facts.

Therefore the current bounded W3 result—exact embedding of a GR vacuum branch—does not by itself
own the complete GR quiet response germ. Charles's intended phrase “indistinguishable from GR at
lab and solar scales” may motivate that strengthening, but it has not yet been formally adopted in
this exact dynamical sense.

## 5. Why no operator scale removes regular local corrections

The GR-leading response has dimension \(L^{-2}\). A curvature polynomial of degree \(n>1\) has
dimension \(L^{-2n}\), so adding it to the GR-leading term requires a coefficient with dimension

\[
L^{2(n-1)}.
\]

Neither \(c_E\) alone nor \(G_{\rm obs}\) without a source/mass attachment supplies that vacuum
length. Physical \(X_{\max}\) remains open and is forbidden as an operator input. Curvature ratios
can restore weight one without a new scale, but they are singular or nondifferentiable at the
quiet origin. Consequently, **within a local finite-jet regular response**, full GR quiet-principal
overlap plus the active no-scale gate closes the response to G301.

## 6. Why locality is independent

There are metric-only, scale-free, quiet-regular nonlocal competitors. A representative type is

\[
u=\Box^{-2}(R_{ab}R^{ab}),
\qquad
E_{ab}=S_{ab}+\alpha\,\operatorname{TF}_g(\nabla_a u\nabla_b u).
\]

Here \(u\) is dimensionless and the correction again has dimension \(L^{-2}\). With a retarded
zero-source prescription it vanishes on Ricci-flat metrics and begins beyond first quiet order, so
the GR quiet principal response survives. But \(\Box^{-2}\) requires a Green function plus
boundary/history data. Two exact discrete histories with identical final local source data give
different terminal integrated carry in both implementations.

This is a counterarchitecture, not a proposed UDT law. It proves that GR principal overlap does not
imply locality. Metric causality also does not settle the issue: a retarded nonlocal response can be
causal while retaining history dependence. The non-signalling co-presence frame makes it especially
important not to assume locality by habit.

## 7. Independence and smallest honest closure

The two witnesses separate the choices:

| Witness | Local | GR solution overlap | GR principal overlap |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Pure curvature quadratic | yes | yes | no |
| Scale-free nonlocal metric history | no | yes | yes |

Thus neither missing clause entails the other. A sentence that simply conjoins them is one written
sentence but two logically independent premises.

The exact conditional theorem is:

> If the vacuum UDT response is a regular local finite-jet natural response of the complete metric,
> has the full GR quiet principal germ, and carries no independent operator scale, then it lies in
> G301; owner-adopted Universal Reciprocity then gives \(R_{ab}-\tfrac14Rg_{ab}=0\).

## 8. What remains open

- whether Charles intends W3 to include full quiet dynamical/principal equivalence;
- whether UDT response locality is a postulate, derivable from causal reciprocity, or intentionally
  relaxed by co-presence;
- the connected scalar's magnitude/sign, lawful data, global history, population, source/mass,
  observations, completion, and physical \(X_{\max}\).

No metric, reciprocal kernel, angular cancellation, or observational interface changed.

Fresh external review independently retained the mathematics and premise ownership. After the
single preregistered sealed-layout repair, the repair-only reviewer reproduced the aggregate replay
and returned `G312_ACCEPTED_WITH_TWO_PREMISE_BOUNDARY`.

FULL_COVARIANT_RECIPROCITY_CLOSES_RESPONSE_SHAPE_ONLY

owner-adopted Universal Reciprocity postulate therefore reaches all nine trace-free metric-shape
directions locally, not merely a radial slice. For any covariant symmetric response `E`, its exact
full-rank consequence is `TF_g(E)=0`, while the common trace survives.

`Ric-(R/4)g=0`; Bianchi makes `R` one connected constant, and the resulting nonlinear covariant
equation has the ordinary two local metric configuration degrees of freedom after constraints and
gauge. A law then defines an evolving solution space; initial data select a history.

The response formula is not selected by covariance or reciprocity alone. A directly recomputed
conformally flat, non-Einstein time-live metric fails Ricci-response DDR while satisfying a
Weyl-quadratic response identically. That counterresponse lies outside G301 and proves the remaining
premise boundary rather than refuting the conditional G301 theorem.

# G309 audit report

Date: 2026-08-31
Status: `EXTERNAL_GPT54_ACCEPTED_WITH_STATED_CAVEATS__REPAIR_FOLLOWUP_ACCEPTED__SCIENTIFIC_LANDING_UNCHANGED`

## Landing

```text
FOUNDED_STRENGTHENED_CHAIN_REMAINS_COMPATIBILITY_ONLY
__ROUND_HOPF_TIME_LIVE_COUNTERFAMILY_SURVIVES
__CONDITIONAL_TRACEFREE_RESIDUAL_CLOSES_POSITIVE_STANDARD_COMPLETION_TO_ONE_SCALE
__HOPF_STRUCTURE_DOES_NOT_OWN_OR_CALIBRATE_THAT_RESIDUAL
```

The recent postulate and Hopf work has not selected a field equation. An exact smooth deformation
of the positive round time-live metric preserves a whole quiet half-history, global hyperbolicity,
compact `S3` slices, both Hopf families, and normalized Hopf time carry, while changing invariant
curvature. The founded/working evaluator and compatibility statements do not reject it.

G301's conditional trace-free Ricci equation does reject the deformation. In this positive round
branch it forces

\[
aa''-a'^2-1=0,
\]

whose complete standard solution is

\[
a(T)=X\cosh((T-T_0)/X).
\]

Accordingly, the conditional branch has no remaining free profile—only one curvature scale, with
time origin removable by isometry. The unresolved question is whether UDT's founding structure
owns that equation. The Hopf results provide downstream consilience, not a derivation of ownership.

See `EXACT_DERIVATION.md` and the three machine-readable evidence files for the bounded proof.

The fresh external reviewer found no scientific defect and independently reproduced the
load-bearing curvature and residual results. It found a medium replay-portability defect because
the production script required unavailable SymPy, plus a low clarification that repository-only
gates were reported rather than sealed-replayed. The preregistered repair replaces the production

observations and does not import a GR field equation into UDT.

In vacuum, the physical response at an event is completely determined by the completed local metric
geometry and finitely many of its derivatives. No independent remote-history or memory variable is
needed after that local metric jet is fixed.

boundary completion, or whole-history admissibility law may determine which local metric occurs.

different vacuum responses solely because of an additional hidden remote-history label.

# G301 audit report

Date: 2026-08-30

## Primary internal landing

```text
TWO_INEQUIVALENT_FULL_METRIC_QUIET_PRINCIPAL_CLASSES_SURVIVE
__GENERIC_RICCI_FLAT_AND_TRACEFREE_RICCI_WITH_ONE_CONSTANT_SCALAR_DATUM
```

Initial fresh review selected `INTERNAL_CERTIFICATION_FAILURE` while retaining the mathematics.
After preregistered repairs R1--R5, the repair-only reviewer returned `ACCEPT_REPAIRS` and found no
remaining defect inside the repair scope.

## What was established

- Differentiability at the flat curvature origin plus exact positive weight-one homogeneity forces
  a candidate two-jet residual to be exactly linear in curvature.
- In the frozen unoriented natural symmetric rank-two lane, the residual is
  `a Ric_ab + b R g_ab`.
- All coefficients with `a != 0` and `a+4b != 0` are related by an invertible trace adjustment and
  form one Ricci-flat residual-equivalence class.
- `a+4b=0` is an inequivalent trace-free Ricci class. Bianchi carry fixes its scalar curvature to
  one connected-region constant but does not force that constant to zero.
- At nonzero quiet frequency the trace-free class reduces to the same Ricci-flat principal
  equations. Its difference is a zero-mode/integration datum, not an extra local polarization.
- The scalar-only and identity-zero strata fail preregistered gates.
- Identity divergence freedom would select the generic class, but remains an unowned G259
  candidate premise.

## Executable evidence

- Production: 169 exact coefficient-grid cases, 7,880 exact generic inversions, 4,000 principal
  covectors, and 27,829 assertions.
- Independent coefficient-strata replay: 12,000 random rational cases and 49,609 assertions,
  importing no production function. This replay is explicitly conditional on the two-term basis.
- Independent full-space basis census: begins with the 20-dimensional algebraic-curvature space
  and an arbitrary 200-component map to symmetric two-tensors. Six Lorentz generators give 1,200
  equivariance rows of rank 198 under two primes; Ricci and scalar-times-metric are two exact
  independent integer null vectors. It passes 53,605 assertions and imports no production code.
- Hostile catches: 12 of 12 registered mutation and semantic catches pass.
- All four registered G301 replays pass under dependency-free `python3 -S`.
- After the current startup block was compacted from 973 to 848 words without changing scientific
  content, the repository-wide harness reports 197 passed and one expected xfail. The package and
  startup surface are green for banking.

## Four evidence gates

1. **Preregistered:** yes, commit `accfc6b9` was pushed before production outcomes.
2. **Full space or justified scope:** exact coefficient-stratum classification inside the bounded
   rank-two two-jet lane; global/nonlocal and other tensor types remain explicitly open.
3. **Independent verification:** the downstream coefficient replay agrees, the repair adds a
   full-space Lorentz-intertwiner census of the load-bearing basis, and a fresh repair-only
   external reviewer reran all registered checks and returned `ACCEPT_REPAIRS`.
4. **Premise audit:** every class hypothesis remains explicitly candidate/free-and-explored; no
   field equation or UDT law was adopted.

## Scientific boundary

The result narrows a local candidate-law class. It neither selects the UDT dynamics nor derives
the loud regimes. It does not alter the metric, complete pair pullback, reciprocal kernel, angular

# G309 exact derivation — strengthened postulates versus history selection

Date: 2026-08-31
Grade: `EXTERNAL_GPT54_ACCEPTED_WITH_STATED_CAVEATS__REPAIR_FOLLOWUP_ACCEPTED__SCIENTIFIC_LANDING_UNCHANGED`

## Bounded landing

```text
FOUNDED_STRENGTHENED_CHAIN_REMAINS_COMPATIBILITY_ONLY
__ROUND_HOPF_TIME_LIVE_COUNTERFAMILY_SURVIVES
__CONDITIONAL_TRACEFREE_RESIDUAL_CLOSES_POSITIVE_STANDARD_COMPLETION_TO_ONE_SCALE
__HOPF_STRUCTURE_DOES_NOT_OWN_OR_CALIBRATE_THAT_RESIDUAL
```

This is preregistered candidate B. The result does not alter the metric or reciprocal kernel and
does not adopt G301 as a UDT field equation.

## 1. The logical question is layered

The current chain contains three mathematically different layers.

1. F1--F4 and W1, W3--W6 specify reciprocal representation, metric readout, quiet reduction,
   universal metric use, projective pair position, and nonsignalling co-presence.
2. G305--G308 conditionally establish a regular positive global completion, intrinsic Hopf
   families, germ reconstruction, and global chirality carry.
3. G301 classifies candidate local metric equations under several explicit extra assumptions.

Only layer 3 contains a nonidentity differential residual. The fact that one member of layer 3 has
an elegant downstream completion does not make that residual a theorem of layers 1--2.

## 2. Exact counterfamily inside the round completed arena

Let

\[
M=\mathbb R_T\times S^3,
\qquad
g_a=-dT^2+a(T)^2\gamma_1,
\qquad a(T)>0,
\]

where \(\gamma_1\) is the unit-round metric. The G305 positive standard completion is

\[
a_0(T)=X\cosh(T/X).
\]

Define

\[
h(T)=
\begin{cases}
0,&T\le0,\\
e^{-X^2/T^2},&T>0,
\end{cases}
\qquad
a_\epsilon(T)=a_0(T)e^{\epsilon h(T)}
\]

for any nonzero dimensionless \(\epsilon\). The standard flat-function theorem gives
\(h^{(n)}(0^+)=0\) for every finite \(n\). Hence \(a_\epsilon\) is smooth, positive, and agrees
with \(a_0\), including every jet, on the entire half-region \(T\le0\).

Both warped products are globally hyperbolic. The coordinate \(T\) is temporal. On any bounded
\(T\) interval, positivity and continuity of \(a\) bound causal spatial speed, while compactness of
\(S^3\) prevents an inextendible causal curve from ending at a finite value of \(T\). Thus every
inextendible causal curve crosses every constant-\(T\) slice exactly once.

This pair preserves more than finite anchors: it preserves a whole open quiet-side history. W3 can
therefore retain the same exact quiet comparator there. W4 uses each single completed metric for
its clocks, rulers, free fall, and cones. W1 and W5 evaluate supplied regular completed relations.
W6 requires response support inside the relevant metric cone but supplies no response equation.
None of these statements produces a residual that rejects \(a_\epsilon\).

## 3. The two histories are geometrically inequivalent

For a closed round warped metric, the temporal and spatial sectional-curvature channels are

\[
K_T=\frac{a''}{a},
\qquad
K_S=\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2},
\]

and the scalar curvature is

\[
\boxed{
R[a]=6\left(\frac{a''}{a}+\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2}\right).
}
\]

For \(a_0\),

\[
K_T=K_S=\frac1{X^2},
\qquad
R[a_0]=\frac{12}{X^2}.
\]

For the preregistered dimensionless witness \(X=1\), \(\epsilon=1/10\), \(T=1\), the exact
deformation gives

\[
R[a_\epsilon]\approx12.7896011305518.
\]

The scalar curvature therefore differs from the constant value of the standard completion. The
metrics are not related by a coordinate change or isometry.

## 4. Hopf kinematics do not reject the deformation

Every positive round \(S^3\) slice admits the two G306 chiral Hopf congruence families. For either
time-independent spatial Hopf field \(K\), the warped connection gives

\[
\nabla_{\partial_T}K=\frac{a'}aK.
\]

Therefore

\[
\boxed{
\nabla_{\partial_T}\left(\frac Ka\right)=0
}
\]

for every positive function \(a(T)\), including \(a_\epsilon\). The topological class and normalized
helicity are likewise insensitive to the overall round-slice scale. G308's connected carry fixes a
single chirality sector once supplied, while its mirror isometry leaves both sectors physically
unselected by the metric alone.

Thus the Hopf results constrain topology, regularity, and coherent carry. They do not constrain the
time profile or curvature magnitude.

## 5. The conditional G301 residual does reject the deformation

The exceptional G301 candidate equation is

\[
S_{ab}=R_{ab}-\frac14R g_{ab}=0.
\]

On the round warped arena its temporal and spatial mixed Ricci eigenvalues agree exactly when

\[
3\frac{a''}{a}
=
\frac{a''}{a}+2\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2}.
\]

Equivalently,

\[
\boxed{
Q[a]=aa''-a'^2-1=0.
}

The standard completion satisfies \(Q[a_0]=0\). At the same preregistered deformed witness,

\[
Q[a_\epsilon]\approx-0.112217073437567,
\]

so the candidate equation rejects the otherwise compatible history.

Define

\[
\kappa=\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2}.
\]

Direct differentiation gives

\[
\kappa'
=
\frac{2a'}{a^3}Q[a].
\]

Hence \(Q=0\) makes \(\kappa\) one constant on a connected solution. In the positive complete
round branch, write \(\kappa=1/X^2\). The full solution is

\[
\boxed{
a(T)=X\cosh\left(\frac{T-T_0}{X}\right),
}

where \(T_0\) is a shift of time origin. Thus, if G301's trace-free residual is provisionally
adopted as the physical equation, this bounded positive standard branch no longer has a free
history function: it has one physical curvature scale \(X\), plus coordinate/isometry freedom.

This is a substantive closure. It remains `CONDITIONAL` because the assumptions selecting the
G301 law class and its exceptional member have not been derived from F1--F4 or W1--W6.

## 6. Exact interpretation of the apparent ontology issue

There are two different questions:

- **Law selection:** why should UDT obey \(S_{ab}=0\), rather than another admissible parent
  equation? This remains open.
- **Solution selection/calibration:** once \(S_{ab}=0\) and the positive complete branch are
  provisionally chosen, which value of \(X\) is realized? That is one finite datum and may be
  calibrated by an independently valid observational anchor.

Ordinary physical theories generally admit many solutions; that is not a defect. The present gap
is narrower and upstream: UDT has not yet shown that the G301 conditional residual is owned by the
founding postulates. Hopf consilience makes that branch more interesting but does not supply the
missing implication.

## 7. What was and was not accomplished

Accomplished:

- distinguished postulate compatibility from candidate dynamics;
- produced an exact smooth, globally hyperbolic counterfamily retaining round `S3` Hopf geometry;
- proved that Hopf carry is blind to arbitrary positive time-dependent scale;
- proved that the conditional trace-free residual removes that functional freedom;
- showed that the positive standard branch then reduces to one curvature scale.

Not accomplished:

- no native derivation of the G301 residual;
- no numerical scale calibration;
- no source, mass, stability, observation, physical chirality, or `X_max` theorem;
- no nonspherical classification or general initial-value theorem;
- no canonization.

## 8. Evidence

- preregistration committed and pushed at `d0a22f9a` before executable or outcome files existed;
- 13 exact symbolic production checks;
- 28 independent sectional-curvature/Ricci-eigenvalue checks with no production import or SymPy;
- four hostile formula mutations caught;
- current 289-row premise registry verifier passed;
- repository regression: 199 passed, one expected xfail.

    24	    negative = {(1, 0, 2), (2, 1, 0), (0, 2, 1)}

    32	def berger_ricci_from_koszul(q: Fraction) -> tuple[list[list[Fraction]], Fraction]:
    33	    """Compute Ricci for SU(2) lengths (1,1,q) from brackets and Koszul."""
    34	    lengths = [Fraction(1), Fraction(1), q]
    35	    structure = [
    36	        [
    37	            [
    38	                Fraction(2) * lengths[k] * epsilon(i, j, k) / (lengths[i] * lengths[j])
    39	                for k in range(3)
    40	            ]
    41	            for j in range(3)
    42	        ]
    43	        for i in range(3)
    44	    ]
    45	    connection = [
    46	        [
    47	            [
    48	                (
    49	                    structure[i][j][k]
    50	                    - structure[j][k][i]
    51	                    + structure[k][i][j]
    52	                )
    53	                / 2
    54	                for k in range(3)
    55	            ]
    56	            for j in range(3)
    57	        ]
    58	        for i in range(3)
    59	    ]
    60
    61	    def riemann(i: int, j: int, k: int, m: int) -> Fraction:
    62	        return sum(
    63	            connection[j][k][n] * connection[i][n][m]
    64	            - connection[i][k][n] * connection[j][n][m]
    65	            - structure[i][j][n] * connection[n][k][m]
    66	            for n in range(3)
    67	        )
    68

    71	        for j in range(3)
    72	    ]
    73	    scalar = sum(ricci[i][i] for i in range(3))
    74	    return ricci, scalar

   105	        nariai_weyl_sq = nariai_riemann_sq - scalar * scalar / 6
   106	        census.require(round_weyl_sq == 0, f"round Weyl lambda={lam}")
   107	        census.require(nariai_weyl_sq == Fraction(16, 3) * lam * lam, f"product Weyl lambda={lam}")
   108	        if lam != 0:
   109	            census.require(nariai_weyl_sq > 0, f"nonzero product Weyl lambda={lam}")
   110	        branch_rows.append(
   111	            {
   112	                "lambda": lam,
   113	                "scalar_R": scalar,
   114	                "round_weyl_squared": round_weyl_sq,
   115	                "product_weyl_squared": nariai_weyl_sq,
   116	            }
   117	        )
   118
   119	    # Constant homothety preserves the equation while changing the scalar magnitude.
   120	    homothety_rows = []
   121	    lam0 = Fraction(3)
   122	    for scale_sq in map(Fraction, (Fraction(1, 4), Fraction(1, 2), 1, 2, 3, 5, 9)):
   123	        scaled_lambda = lam0 / scale_sq
   124	        scaled_scalar = 4 * scaled_lambda
   125	        census.require(scaled_scalar * scale_sq == 4 * lam0, f"homothety response {scale_sq}")
   126	        census.require(scaled_lambda != 0, f"homothety nonzero {scale_sq}")
   127	        homothety_rows.append(
   128	            {"metric_multiplier": scale_sq, "lambda_after": scaled_lambda, "R_after": scaled_scalar}
   129	        )
   130
   131	    # Positive fixed-Lambda compact Cauchy data: round S3, Nariai S1xS2, and nonround Berger S3.
   132	    lam = Fraction(3)
   133	    cauchy_rows = []
   134	    for name, scalar_3, h_sq, topology, round_state in (
   135	        ("round_S3_bounce", Fraction(6), Fraction(0), "S3", True),
   136	        ("nariai_S1xS2_bounce", Fraction(6), Fraction(0), "S1xS2", False),
   137	    ):
   138	        hamiltonian = scalar_3 + 6 * h_sq
   139	        census.require(hamiltonian == 2 * lam, f"Hamiltonian {name}")
   140	        cauchy_rows.append(
   141	            {
   142	                "name": name,
   143	                "topology": topology,
   144	                "round": round_state,
   145	                "R3": scalar_3,
   146	                "H_squared": h_sq,
   147	                "hamiltonian": hamiltonian,
   148	                "momentum": 0,
   149	            }
   150	        )
   151
   152	    q = Fraction(3, 2)
   153	    berger_ricci, berger_scalar = berger_ricci_from_koszul(q)
   154	    expected_diagonal = [Fraction(-1, 2), Fraction(-1, 2), Fraction(9, 2)]
   155	    for i in range(3):
   156	        for j in range(3):
   157	            expected = expected_diagonal[i] if i == j else Fraction(0)
   158	            census.require(berger_ricci[i][j] == expected, f"Berger Ricci {i}{j}")
   159	    census.require(berger_scalar == Fraction(7, 2), "Berger scalar")
   160	    census.require(len(set(expected_diagonal)) > 1, "Berger data are nonround")
   161	    berger_h_sq = (2 * lam - berger_scalar) / 6
   162	    census.require(berger_h_sq == Fraction(5, 12), "Berger H squared")
   163	    census.require(berger_scalar + 6 * berger_h_sq == 2 * lam, "Berger Hamiltonian")
   164	    cauchy_rows.append(
   165	        {
   166	            "name": "berger_S3_pure_trace",
   167	            "topology": "S3",
   168	            "round": False,
   169	            "R3": berger_scalar,
   170	            "H_squared": berger_h_sq,
   171	            "hamiltonian": berger_scalar + 6 * berger_h_sq,
   172	            "momentum": 0,
   173	            "ricci_eigenvalues": expected_diagonal,
   174	        }
   175	    )
   176
   177	    # Exact rational hyperbolic points verify the G309 cosh branch equation.
   178	    cosh_checks = []
   179	    for u in map(Fraction, (Fraction(1, 3), Fraction(1, 2), Fraction(2, 3), 1, 2, 3, 5)):
   180	        cosh_value = (u + 1 / u) / 2
   181	        sinh_value = (1 / u - u) / 2
   182	        residual = cosh_value * cosh_value - sinh_value * sinh_value - 1
   183	        census.require(residual == 0, f"cosh branch u={u}")
   184	        cosh_checks.append({"u": u, "dimensionless_residual": residual})
   185
   186	    # Ricci-flat plane-wave witness: H=A(x^2-y^2) has vanishing transverse Laplacian but nonzero tide.
   187	    plane_wave_rows = []
   188	    for amplitude in map(Fraction, (-5, -2, -1, 1, 2, 5)):
   189	        h_xx = 2 * amplitude
   190	        h_yy = -2 * amplitude
   191	        ricci_uu = -(h_xx + h_yy) / 2
   192	        census.require(ricci_uu == 0, f"plane-wave Ricci amplitude={amplitude}")
   193	        census.require(h_xx - h_yy != 0, f"plane-wave tide amplitude={amplitude}")
   194	        plane_wave_rows.append(
   195	            {"amplitude": amplitude, "Ric_uu": ricci_uu, "tidal_difference": h_xx - h_yy}
   196	        )
   197
   198	    # Type separation: a global predicate can choose histories without changing local response.
   199	    local_response_signature = "Ric_ab-(R/4)g_ab"
   200	    round_history = {"local_response": local_response_signature, "global_topology": "R_x_S3"}
   201	    product_history = {"local_response": local_response_signature, "global_topology": "R_x_S1_x_S2"}
   202	    census.require(
   203	        round_history["local_response"] == product_history["local_response"],
   204	        "same local response law",
   205	    )
   206	    census.require(
   207	        round_history["global_topology"] != product_history["global_topology"],
   208	        "global predicate can distinguish histories",
   209	    )
   210
   211	    result = {
   212	        "status": "PASS",
   213	        "landing": "ACTIVE_EQUATION_DEFINES_MULTIBRANCH_EINSTEIN_ARENA__GLOBAL_ADMISSIBILITY_REMAINS_OPEN",
   214	        "assertions": census.checks,
   215	        "bianchi_divergence_coefficient": divergence_coefficient,
   216	        "equivalent_connected_equation": "Ric_ab=Lambda*g_ab; dLambda=0",
   217	        "branch_rows": branch_rows,
   218	        "homothety_rows": homothety_rows,
   219	        "cauchy_rows": cauchy_rows,
   220	        "cosh_checks": cosh_checks,
   221	        "plane_wave_rows": plane_wave_rows,
   222	        "global_predicate_type": "diffeomorphism-invariant acceptance predicate on complete histories",
   223	        "existing_nonidentity_global_predicate_owned": False,
   224	        "round_history_selected": False,
   225	        "scale_selected": False,
   226	        "physical_xmax_selected": False,
   227	    }
   228	    encoded = encode(result)

   230	    if args.output:
   231	        args.output.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8")
   232	    else:

    12	def encode(value):
    13	    if isinstance(value, Fraction):
    14	        return str(value)
    15	    if isinstance(value, dict):
    16	        return {key: encode(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    17	    if isinstance(value, list):
    18	        return [encode(item) for item in value]
    19	    return value
    20
    21
    22	def main() -> None:
    23	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    24	    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    25	    args = parser.parse_args()
    26
    27	    checks = []
    28
    29	    def require(condition: bool, label: str) -> None:
    30	        if not condition:
    31	            raise AssertionError(label)
    32	        checks.append(label)
    33
    34	    # Independent trace/divergence route in n dimensions, specialized only after derivation.
    35	    for n in (3, 4, 5, 7):
    36	        coefficient = Fraction(1, 2) - Fraction(1, n)
    37	        require(coefficient == Fraction(n - 2, 2 * n), f"Bianchi n={n}")
    38	    require(Fraction(1, 2) - Fraction(1, 4) == Fraction(1, 4), "four-dimensional constancy")
    39
    40	    # Product-space route: each two-factor has Ric=k*g and curvature norm 4*k^2.
    41	    product_rows = []
    42	    for k in map(Fraction, (-7, -3, -1, 1, 3, 7)):
    43	        scalar = 4 * k
    44	        riemann_sq = 4 * k * k + 4 * k * k
    45	        weyl_sq = riemann_sq - scalar * scalar / 6
    46	        require(weyl_sq == Fraction(16, 3) * k * k, f"product Weyl k={k}")
    47	        require(weyl_sq != 0, f"product nonconformal k={k}")
    48	        product_rows.append({"k": k, "R": scalar, "Riemann_squared": riemann_sq, "Weyl_squared": weyl_sq})
    49
    50	    # Closed Berger formulas provide a route independent of the production Koszul implementation.
    51	    berger_rows = []
    52	    for q in map(Fraction, (Fraction(1, 2), 1, Fraction(4, 3), Fraction(3, 2), 2)):
    53	        horizontal = 4 - 2 * q * q
    54	        vertical = 2 * q * q
    55	        scalar = 2 * horizontal + vertical
    56	        require(scalar == 8 - 2 * q * q, f"Berger scalar q={q}")
    57	        if q == 1:
    58	            require(horizontal == vertical, "round Berger q=1")
    59	        else:
    60	            require(horizontal != vertical, f"nonround Berger q={q}")
    61	        berger_rows.append(
    62	            {"q": q, "horizontal_Ricci": horizontal, "vertical_Ricci": vertical, "R3": scalar}
    63	        )
    64
    65	    # Two distinct compact fixed-Lambda initial data sets satisfy the Hamiltonian constraint.
    66	    lam = Fraction(3)
    67	    initial_rows = [
    68	        ("round_S3", Fraction(6), Fraction(0)),
    69	        ("product_S1xS2", Fraction(6), Fraction(0)),
    70	        ("berger_S3", Fraction(7, 2), Fraction(5, 12)),
    71	    ]
    72	    for name, scalar_3, h_sq in initial_rows:
    73	        require(scalar_3 + 6 * h_sq == 2 * lam, f"constraint {name}")
    74
    75	    # Coordinate 2D de Sitter factor: a''=a gives Ric_tt=g_tt and Ric_xx=g_xx at unit radius.
    76	    for u in map(Fraction, (Fraction(1, 4), Fraction(1, 2), 1, 2, 4)):
    77	        a = (u + 1 / u) / 2
    78	        adot = (1 / u - u) / 2
    79	        addot = a
    80	        require(-addot / a == -1, f"dS2 Ric_tt u={u}")
    81	        require(a * addot == a * a, f"dS2 Ric_xx u={u}")
    82	        require(a * a - adot * adot == 1, f"dS2 hyperbola u={u}")
    83
    84	    # Homothety is checked through the response tensor, not only through scalar dimensions.
    85	    signature = [-1, 1, 1, 1]
    86	    for metric_multiplier in map(Fraction, (Fraction(1, 3), Fraction(1, 2), 2, 4, 7)):
    87	        lam_before = Fraction(5)
    88	        ricci_lower = [lam_before * item for item in signature]
    89	        metric_after = [metric_multiplier * item for item in signature]
    90	        scalar_after = 4 * lam_before / metric_multiplier
    91	        response_after = [
    92	            ricci_lower[i] - scalar_after * metric_after[i] / 4 for i in range(4)
    93	        ]
    94	        require(response_after == [0, 0, 0, 0], f"homothety tensor {metric_multiplier}")
    95
    96	    # Plane-wave route: trace-free Hessian gives Ricci zero but a nonzero tidal Hessian.
    97	    for amplitude in map(Fraction, (-4, -1, 1, 4)):
    98	        hessian = [[2 * amplitude, 0], [0, -2 * amplitude]]
    99	        trace = hessian[0][0] + hessian[1][1]
   100	        determinant = hessian[0][0] * hessian[1][1]
   101	        require(trace == 0, f"plane trace {amplitude}")
   102	        require(determinant < 0, f"plane nonzero tide {amplitude}")
   103
   104	    result = {
   105	        "status": "PASS",
   106	        "implementation": "independent_closed_formula_and_tensor_route",
   107	        "assertions": len(checks),
   108	        "product_rows": product_rows,
   109	        "berger_rows": berger_rows,

   112	        "round_uniqueness_refuted": True,
   113	        "scale_selection_refuted": True,

     6	## 1. The adopted bounded equation
     7
     8	Write
     9
    10	\[
    11	S_{ab}=R_{ab}-\frac14R g_{ab}.
    12	\]
    13
    14	The owner-adopted provisional premises activate `S_ab=0` only inside G312's registered bounded
    15	arena. Contracted Bianchi gives
    16
    17	\[
    18	\nabla^aS_{ab}
    19	=\left(\frac12-\frac14\right)\nabla_bR
    20	=\frac14\nabla_bR=0.
    21	\]
    22
    23	Therefore, on each connected regular solution region,
    24
    25	\[
    26	R=4\Lambda,\qquad d\Lambda=0,\qquad R_{ab}=\Lambda g_{ab}.
    27	\]
    28
    29	This fixes a local vacuum response law. It does not fix the value of `Lambda`, Cauchy data, global
    30	topology, or which solution is physically populated.
    31
    32	## 2. Constant curvature is a proper subset
    33
    34	For a four-dimensional Einstein metric,
    35
    36	\[
    37	R_{abcd}=C_{abcd}
    38	+\frac{\Lambda}{3}(g_{ac}g_{bd}-g_{ad}g_{bc}).
    39	\]
    40
    41	The equation fixes the Ricci channel but leaves the Weyl tensor `C_abcd` free subject to its own
    42	geometric identities and evolution. Only the subfamily `C_abcd=0` has constant sectional curvature.
    43	Thus trace-free Ricci does not imply that every solution is round de Sitter, flat Minkowski, or
    44	anti-de Sitter.
    45
    46	For the constant-curvature subfamily,
    47
    48	\[
    49	R_{abcd}R^{abcd}=\frac83\Lambda^2,
    50	\qquad C_{abcd}C^{abcd}=0.
    51	\]
    52

    54

    56	a(T)=X\cosh\!\left(\frac{T-T_0}{X}\right),
    57	\qquad \Lambda=\frac3{X^2},
    58	\]

    60	is one exact member of this conformally flat subfamily.

    65
    66	\[
    67	ds^2=\Lambda^{-1}

    69	\qquad \chi\sim\chi+2\pi.
    70	\]
    71
    72	Each two-dimensional factor has `Ric=Lambda g`, so the product has

    75	R_{ab}=\Lambda g_{ab},\qquad R=4\Lambda.

     1	# G309 exact derivation — strengthened postulates versus history selection
     2
     3	Date: 2026-08-31

    15	This is preregistered candidate B. The result does not alter the metric or reciprocal kernel and
    16	does not adopt G301 as a UDT field equation.
    17
    18	## 1. The logical question is layered
    19
    20	The current chain contains three mathematically different layers.
    21
    22	1. F1--F4 and W1, W3--W6 specify reciprocal representation, metric readout, quiet reduction,
    23	   universal metric use, projective pair position, and nonsignalling co-presence.
    24	2. G305--G308 conditionally establish a regular positive global completion, intrinsic Hopf

    26	3. G301 classifies candidate local metric equations under several explicit extra assumptions.
    27
    28	Only layer 3 contains a nonidentity differential residual. The fact that one member of layer 3 has
    29	an elegant downstream completion does not make that residual a theorem of layers 1--2.
    30
    31	## 2. Exact counterfamily inside the round completed arena
    32
    33	Let
    34
    35	\[
    36	M=\mathbb R_T\times S^3,
    37	\qquad
    38	g_a=-dT^2+a(T)^2\gamma_1,
    39	\qquad a(T)>0,
    40	\]
    41
    42	where \(\gamma_1\) is the unit-round metric. The G305 positive standard completion is
    43
    44	\[
    45	a_0(T)=X\cosh(T/X).
    46	\]
    47
    48	Define
    49
    50	\[
    51	h(T)=
    52	\begin{cases}
    53	0,&T\le0,\\
    54	e^{-X^2/T^2},&T>0,
    55	\end{cases}
    56	\qquad
    57	a_\epsilon(T)=a_0(T)e^{\epsilon h(T)}
    58	\]
    59
    60	for any nonzero dimensionless \(\epsilon\). The standard flat-function theorem gives
    61	\(h^{(n)}(0^+)=0\) for every finite \(n\). Hence \(a_\epsilon\) is smooth, positive, and agrees
    62	with \(a_0\), including every jet, on the entire half-region \(T\le0\).
    63
    64	Both warped products are globally hyperbolic. The coordinate \(T\) is temporal. On any bounded
    65	\(T\) interval, positivity and continuity of \(a\) bound causal spatial speed, while compactness of
    66	\(S^3\) prevents an inextendible causal curve from ending at a finite value of \(T\). Thus every
    67	inextendible causal curve crosses every constant-\(T\) slice exactly once.
    68
    69	This pair preserves more than finite anchors: it preserves a whole open quiet-side history. W3 can
    70	therefore retain the same exact quiet comparator there. W4 uses each single completed metric for
    71	its clocks, rulers, free fall, and cones. W1 and W5 evaluate supplied regular completed relations.
    72	W6 requires response support inside the relevant metric cone but supplies no response equation.
    73	None of these statements produces a residual that rejects \(a_\epsilon\).
    74
    75	## 3. The two histories are geometrically inequivalent
    76
    77	For a closed round warped metric, the temporal and spatial sectional-curvature channels are
    78
    79	\[
    80	K_T=\frac{a''}{a},
    81	\qquad
    82	K_S=\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2},
    83	\]
    84
    85	and the scalar curvature is
    86
    87	\[
    88	\boxed{
    89	R[a]=6\left(\frac{a''}{a}+\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2}\right).
    90	}
    91	\]
    92
    93	For \(a_0\),
    94
    95	\[
    96	K_T=K_S=\frac1{X^2},
    97	\qquad
    98	R[a_0]=\frac{12}{X^2}.
    99	\]
   100
   101	For the preregistered dimensionless witness \(X=1\), \(\epsilon=1/10\), \(T=1\), the exact
   102	deformation gives
   103
   104	\[
   105	R[a_\epsilon]\approx12.7896011305518.
   106	\]
   107
   108	The scalar curvature therefore differs from the constant value of the standard completion. The
   109	metrics are not related by a coordinate change or isometry.
   110
   111	## 4. Hopf kinematics do not reject the deformation
   112
   113	Every positive round \(S^3\) slice admits the two G306 chiral Hopf congruence families. For either
   114	time-independent spatial Hopf field \(K\), the warped connection gives
   115
   116	\[
   117	\nabla_{\partial_T}K=\frac{a'}aK.
   118	\]
   119
   120	Therefore
   121
   122	\[
   123	\boxed{
   124	\nabla_{\partial_T}\left(\frac Ka\right)=0
   125	}
   126	\]
   127
   128	for every positive function \(a(T)\), including \(a_\epsilon\). The topological class and normalized
   129	helicity are likewise insensitive to the overall round-slice scale. G308's connected carry fixes a
   130	single chirality sector once supplied, while its mirror isometry leaves both sectors physically
   131	unselected by the metric alone.
   132
   133	Thus the Hopf results constrain topology, regularity, and coherent carry. They do not constrain the
   134	time profile or curvature magnitude.
   135
   136	## 5. The conditional G301 residual does reject the deformation
   137
   138	The exceptional G301 candidate equation is
   139
   140	\[
   141	S_{ab}=R_{ab}-\frac14R g_{ab}=0.
   142	\]
   143
   144	On the round warped arena its temporal and spatial mixed Ricci eigenvalues agree exactly when
   145
   146	\[
   147	3\frac{a''}{a}
   148	=
   149	\frac{a''}{a}+2\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2}.
   150	\]
   151
   152	Equivalently,
   153
   154	\[
   155	\boxed{
   156	Q[a]=aa''-a'^2-1=0.
   157	}
   158
   159	The standard completion satisfies \(Q[a_0]=0\). At the same preregistered deformed witness,
   160
   161	\[
   162	Q[a_\epsilon]\approx-0.112217073437567,
   163	\]
   164
   165	so the candidate equation rejects the otherwise compatible history.
   166
   167	Define
   168
   169	\[
   170	\kappa=\frac{a'^2+1}{a^2}.
   171	\]
   172
   173	Direct differentiation gives
   174
   175	\[
   176	\kappa'
   177	=
   178	\frac{2a'}{a^3}Q[a].
   179	\]
   180
   181	Hence \(Q=0\) makes \(\kappa\) one constant on a connected solution. In the positive complete
   182	round branch, write \(\kappa=1/X^2\). The full solution is
   183
   184	\[
   185	\boxed{
   186	a(T)=X\cosh\left(\frac{T-T_0}{X}\right),
   187	}
   188
   189	where \(T_0\) is a shift of time origin. Thus, if G301's trace-free residual is provisionally
   190	adopted as the physical equation, this bounded positive standard branch no longer has a free
   191	history function: it has one physical curvature scale \(X\), plus coordinate/isometry freedom.
   192
   193	This is a substantive closure. It remains `CONDITIONAL` because the assumptions selecting the
   194	G301 law class and its exceptional member have not been derived from F1--F4 or W1--W6.
   195
   196	## 6. Exact interpretation of the apparent ontology issue
   197
   198	There are two different questions:
   199
   200	- **Law selection:** why should UDT obey \(S_{ab}=0\), rather than another admissible parent
   201	  equation? This remains open.
   202	- **Solution selection/calibration:** once \(S_{ab}=0\) and the positive complete branch are
   203	  provisionally chosen, which value of \(X\) is realized? That is one finite datum and may be
   204	  calibrated by an independently valid observational anchor.
   205
   206	Ordinary physical theories generally admit many solutions; that is not a defect. The present gap
   207	is narrower and upstream: UDT has not yet shown that the G301 conditional residual is owned by the
   208	founding postulates. Hopf consilience makes that branch more interesting but does not supply the
   209	missing implication.
   210
   211	## 7. What was and was not accomplished
   212
   213	Accomplished:
   214
   215	- distinguished postulate compatibility from candidate dynamics;
   216	- produced an exact smooth, globally hyperbolic counterfamily retaining round `S3` Hopf geometry;
   217	- proved that Hopf carry is blind to arbitrary positive time-dependent scale;
   218	- proved that the conditional trace-free residual removes that functional freedom;
   219	- showed that the positive standard branch then reduces to one curvature scale.
   220
   221	Not accomplished:
   222
   223	- no native derivation of the G301 residual;
   224	- no numerical scale calibration;
   225	- no source, mass, stability, observation, physical chirality, or `X_max` theorem;
   226	- no nonspherical classification or general initial-value theorem;
   227	- no canonization.
